## Corpus Check
- 1525 files · ~2,669,285 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 14016 nodes · 16082 edges · 1399 communities (1312 shown, 87 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 100% EXTRACTED · 0% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 43 edges (avg confidence: 0.7)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5a78ef34`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
